### [1] [From Optimal Policies to Individual Differences: Rethinking Reinforcement Learning for Biology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16542)
*Patrick Govoni,Palina Bartashevich,Clémence Bergerot,Valerii Chirkov,Valentin Lecheval,Pawel Romanczuk*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强化学习（RL）在模拟生物行为时的一个关键缺陷——缺乏个体间的行为变异性。虽然RL在捕捉生物学核心特征方面表现出色，但其模型通常只呈现单一最优个体或群体平均水平，忽略了生物群体中普遍存在的个体差异。作者考察了RL各子领域中的方法，并提出了弥合生物学与仿真之间差距的潜在路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL模型在解释生物行为时，忽略了生物群体中普遍存在的个体间行为差异性，通常只输出单一最优解或群体平均值，这与生物现实严重不符。作者希望推动RL模型向能够生成具有生物可信机制的行为多样性方向发展。

Method: 作者从RL的多个子领域（如多智能体RL、分层RL、内在动机RL等）中审视现有方法，分析哪些机制可能用于生成个体间行为变异性，并提出融合这些方法的潜在路径。

Result: 指出了当前RL模型在模拟生物行为多样性方面的不足，并识别了多个子领域中可用于生成行为变异性的候选方法。

Conclusion: 弥合生物行为与RL仿真之间的差距需要超越当前实践，采用更具生物合理性的机制来生成行为多样性，使RL模型更好地反映生物群体的真实特征。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is primarily known as a computational method for optimizing control tasks, but it is increasingly used to explain biological behavior. While RL successfully captures key aspects of biology, a major gap remains: between-agent behavioral variability. Consistent individual differences naturally permeate biological populations, yet RL models typically present only the single best individual or the population average. Addressing this gap requires moving beyond current practices to generate behavioral diversity using biologically plausible mechanisms. Here, we examine approaches from various subfields of RL and outline potential paths forward to close the gap between biology and simulation.

</details>


### [2] [How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: From Thagard's Brain--Mind Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16573)
*Hiroyuki Chuma,Kanji Otsuka,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出VaCoAl超维计算架构，通过GF(2)上的XOR与移位操作实现精确可逆绑定，同时满足Marcus的变量-结构-个体三支柱和Thagard的绑定需求，解决传统卷积在深度递归下的退化问题，并以齿状回-CA3回路作为收敛进化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marcus的代数心智指出架构需支持变量操作、结构化表示和个体与类别分离，但感知机无法实现；Thagard的脑-心智理论将绑定作为组装感知、情绪、意识和自我的单一机制，但依赖的循环卷积是有损代数，在递归下退化。两个独立认知架构批判共同指向一个缺失能力——可逆精确绑定。

Method: 提出VaCoAl超维计算架构，核心原语为GF(2)域上的XOR-and-shift操作（基于本原多项式线性反馈移位寄存器）。绑定操作 Bind(R,F)=R⊕shift(F) 是精确可逆且非交换的。实现为PyVaCoAl，包含多级救援电路、百万维度规模和Rescue-Rate相变。

Result: 该架构精确可逆绑定满足Marcus的三支柱——开放寄存器代数（支柱1）、固定维度组合捆绑（支柱2）、个体与类别分离（支柱3）；消除了Thagard最深递归下卷积的深度退化问题。O(N)复杂度的精确可逆绑定支持组合泛化与事后可审计性。齿状回-CA3生物回路要求相同的可逆组合代数，提供趋同进化证据。

Conclusion: VaCoAl作为统计嵌入缺失的可逆、可审计、多跳关系推理的正交基板，不与LLM竞争但互补。生物神经回路与两个独立认知架构程序共同要求该代数，表明其具有基板无关性。

Abstract: Two critiques of connectionist cognition converge on one missing capacity. In The Algebraic Mind, Marcus isolated three components any architecture must support -- operations over variables, structured representations, and individuals distinct from kinds -- showed perceptrons support none, and left neural implementation as conjecture. In Brain-Mind, Thagard made binding the single mechanism assembling perception, emotion, consciousness, and self, but rested it on circular convolution, a lossy algebra that degrades under the recursion his account of self and emotion demands.
  We argue one substrate answers both. VaCoAl is a hyperdimensional architecture built on one primitive, XOR-and-shift over GF(2), on primitive-polynomial linear-feedback shift registers; PyVaCoAl is its extended realization, adding a multi-stage rescue circuit, million-dimensional scale, and a Rescue-Rate phase transition (SRAM-CAM hardware and low-power claim remain future work). Binding, Bind(R,F) = R $\oplus$ shift(F), is exactly reversible and non-commutative: it fills Marcus's open register algebra (Pillar 1), supports compositional bundling at fixed dimension (Pillar 2), and separates individuals from kinds (Pillar 3), while removing the depth-degradation that afflicts convolution at Thagard's deepest recursions.
  We make three scoped claims. Capability: exact reversible binding at O(N) yields compositional generalization with post-hoc auditability no lossy or learned substrate offers. Necessity: two independent cognitive-architecture programs and a biological circuit (dentate gyrus-CA3) requiring the same reversible-compositional algebra is evidence, by convergent evolution not biomimicry, that it is substrate-independent. Position: we do not claim to surpass LLMs; the substrate is orthogonal, supplying the reversible, auditable, multi-hop relational reasoning statistical embeddings structurally lack.

</details>


### [3] [How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: From Minsky's Emotion-Machine Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16623)
*Hiroyuki Chuma,Kanji Otsuka,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出VaCoAl架构，一种基于GF(2)异或移位操作的精确可逆变量绑定方案，并通过Minsky《情感机器》的反思层框架，论证只有精确可逆代数（如VaCoAl）才能实现真实的反思性内省，区别于概率性（LLM）和近似代数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marcus在《代数心智》中提出了三种认知成分（变量操作、递归结构表示、个体/种类区分），但未指定神经实现。本文旨在为VaCoAl架构提供认知垂直维度（心智对自身的推理）的理论支撑，填补Marcus水平框架与Minsky反思层之间的理论空白。

Method: 将VaCoAl的精确可逆绑定与Minsky《情感机器》的反思层（Reflective layer）对应，提出反思性操作即对自己推理轨迹的可逆解绑。将内省可信度划分为三个层级：概率性（LLM）、近似代数、精确代数（VaCoAl），并论证只有最后者能保证反思性恢复的真实性。进一步将该机制扩展到Minsky的panalogy（类比检索）和信用分配（反事实模拟）。

Result: 证明VaCoAl的精确可逆性直接支持内省操作（反思层），区分了痕迹执行与理论反思论证，指出元控制回路留待未来工作。

Conclusion: VaCoAl的精确可逆代数不仅支撑Pearl因果层次攀登，也支撑Minsky内省栈的上升，两者在此相交。但需注意，论文仅分离论证了痕迹执行与理论反思，元控制回路未实现。

Abstract: In The Algebraic Mind, Marcus identified three cognitive components: operations over variables, recursively structured representations, and an individual/kind distinction. He left the neural substrate open. A companion paper solves this with VaCoAl, an architecture built on GF(2) XOR-and-shift. It offers exact reversible variable binding, compositional bundling, and spatial individual/kind separation. A collision-avoidance scheme ensures exact recovery within a Frontier Size. Its PyVaCoAl implementation demonstrates this algebra scales to millions of records, while the SRAM-CAM version favors hardware speed. Marcus's horizontal framework concerns how a mind represents the world. This paper reads VaCoAl through an orthogonal lens: Minsky's The Emotion Machine, providing the vertical dimension of a mind reasoning about itself. We claim VaCoAl's exact preservation and recovery of structural representations directly enables introspection -- Minsky's Reflective layer. Reflexively unbinding one's own deliberative trace is the operation. We grade deliberation into three tiers by trace faithfulness: probabilistic (LLMs), approximate-algebraic, and exact-algebraic (VaCoAl). Only exact reversibility ensures reflexive recovery is faithful rather than spurious, reaching the Reflective layer. This substrate carries two more Minsky ideas: panalogy (achieved via content-addressable retrieval where match accuracy indicates analogical fit, enabling lifelong learning) and credit assignment (framed as reflexive counterfactual simulation: surgically unbinding a past choice and rebinding a counterfactual to compare). The exact reversibility allowing the companion paper to climb Pearl's causal ladder helps this paper ascend Minsky's introspective stack, finding the intersection. We separate demonstrated trace execution from theoretical Reflection arguments, leaving meta-control loops strictly for future work.

</details>


### [4] [Decision Variable Analysis-Guided Differentiated Fuzzy Search for Large-Scale Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16723)
*Boxi Xiao,Hui Bai,Jinhua Zheng,Yu Li,Juan Zou*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种决策变量分析引导的差异化模糊搜索方法(DDFS)，通过建立变量角色与搜索粒度的显式映射，并设计双指标阶段转换机制，在大规模多目标优化问题上取得了竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊搜索方法对所有变量采用统一搜索粒度，忽略了不同角色变量在促进收敛和维持多样性方面的异质搜索需求。

Method: 1) 建立决策变量角色与模糊搜索粒度的显式映射；2) 利用决策变量分析识别变量角色和搜索敏感性，使不同变量组在子代生成中采用差异化模糊搜索行为；3) 提出双指标阶段转换机制，动态调整模糊更新强度，平衡早期搜索空间压缩与后期收敛精炼。

Result: 在最多1000维的LSMOP和UF基准测试集上，DDFS相较于多个代表性大规模多目标进化算法取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 将决策变量角色显式融入模糊搜索有助于改善高维决策空间中的优化性能。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-objective optimization problems (LSMOPs) are challenging due to their high-dimensional decision spaces. Fuzzy search is an effective technique for improving search efficiency, while decision variable analysis can reveal the distinct roles of variables in promoting convergence and maintaining diversity. However, existing fuzzy search methods generally employ a uniform search granularity for all variables, overlooking the heterogeneous search requirements implied by variable roles. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a Decision variable analysis-guided Differentiated Fuzzy Search method, termed DDFS. The proposed method establishes an explicit mapping between decision-variable roles and fuzzy search granularities. Decision variable analysis is employed to identify variable roles and search sensitivities, enabling different variable groups to adopt differentiated fuzzy search behaviors during offspring generation. Furthermore, a Dual-Indicator Stage Transition Mechanism is developed to dynamically adjust fuzzy-updating intensity throughout the evolutionary process, balancing early-stage search-space compression and late-stage convergence refinement. Extensive experiments on the LSMOP and UF benchmark suites with up to 1000 decision variables show that DDFS generally achieves competitive performance against several representative large-scale multi-objective evolutionary algorithms. The results suggest that explicitly incorporating decision-variable roles into fuzzy search can help improve optimization performance in high-dimensional decision spaces.

</details>


### [5] [Hybrid Augmented Lagrangian Method for General Constrained Optimization via Evolutionary Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16876)
*Lampros Printzios,Konstantinos Chatzilygeroudis*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合增广拉格朗日（HyAL）方法，将增广拉格朗日框架的约束处理能力与种群搜索的探索能力相结合，在基准优化问题上优于纯进化方法和数值优化方法，尤其在高维复杂约束问题上表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化方法（如可行方向法、内点法、序列二次规划）需要精确解析梯度和有效初始化，实际应用中面临挑战；进化算法无需梯度、鲁棒性强但计算成本高、收敛慢。因此，需要一种结合两者优势的方法。

Method: HyAL方法将增广拉格朗日（AL）框架与进化算法相结合：利用AL框架处理约束，同时采用进化技术在AL迭代中求解子问题，促进探索并帮助逃离局部最优。实验对比了IPOPT、CMA-ES以及纯进化基线方法，并在AL框架内评估了四种不同的种群方法。

Result: HyAL在基准测试中持续产生高质量解，优于纯进化方法，在高维约束问题上扩展性更好；在含有大量局部极小值和鞍点的复杂景观上，HyAL也超越了最先进的数值优化算法。

Conclusion: HyAL成功融合了增广拉格朗日框架的约束处理能力和进化算法的探索能力，在复杂约束优化问题中兼具高效性与鲁棒性，是一种有前景的混合优化方法。

Abstract: Constrained Optimization Problems are crucial in fields such as engineering, economics, and robotics, where high-dimensional search spaces and complex objectives and constraints are common. Numerical optimization methods, including Feasible Direction, Interior Point, and Sequential Quadratic Programming, have shown strong performance in finding feasible local optima, but require accurate analytical gradients and effective initialization, which can be challenging in real-world settings. Evolutionary Algorithms, on the other hand, offer gradient-free search and robustness to noisy landscapes, managing to detect global optima more often than numerical methods, but they often suffer from high computational costs and slow convergence. In this work, we propose a Hybrid Augmented Lagrangian (HyAL) method that integrates the AL framework's constraint-handling strengths with the exploratory power of population-based search. Our approach employs evolutionary techniques to solve subproblems within the AL iterations, promoting exploration and aiding in the escape from local optima. We conduct extensive experiments on benchmark optimization problems, comparing our method against state-of-the-art optimizers, including IPOPT and CMA-ES, and a standalone evolutionary optimization baseline (with constraint enforcement via penalties). In addition, we evaluate four population-based methods integrated within the AL framework to study the effect of different evolutionary solvers. Our results show that HyAL consistently produces high-quality solutions across the benchmark suite. It outperforms purely evolutionary approaches and scales more effectively to high-dimensional constrained problems, where evolutionary-only methods often struggle. HyAL also surpasses state-of-the-art numerical optimization algorithms on complex landscapes containing numerous local minima and saddle points.

</details>


### [6] [Organization of computation in reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17858)
*Mohab Abdalla,Damien Rontani*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种特征谱分解框架，将信息处理容量按状态空间模态分解，发现任务相关信息可能分布在低能量模态中，易受实验噪声影响。这表明储层计算不仅依赖维度扩展，还依赖任务相关信息的几何组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的储层计算性能评估多通过记忆、非线性及其权衡等聚合性指标，但这些指标无法揭示任务相关信息在状态空间中的具体组织方式。作者希望引入一种框架，将信息处理容量按状态空间模态进行分解，从而量化不同模态对信息处理的贡献。

Method: 引入特征谱分解（eigen-spectral decomposition）框架，将度级（degree-wise）信息处理容量与对应的状态空间模态联系起来，进而量化度级表示能量（degree-wise representation energy）。

Result: 发现部分情况下，大量信息处理容量可能分布在低能量模态中，而这些低能量模态对实验噪声更为敏感。

Conclusion: 有用的储层计算不仅依赖于维度扩展，还依赖于任务相关信息在状态空间中的几何组织方式，这对物理储层计算具有直接影响。

Abstract: Reservoir computing exploits nonlinear dynamical systems to encode temporal inputs into high-dimensional state space representations. Although reservoir performance is often characterized through memory, nonlinearity, and their tradeoff, such aggregate measures do not reveal how task-relevant information is organized within the state space. Here, we introduce an eigen-spectral decomposition framework linking the degree-wise information processing capacity to the corresponding state space modes. As a result, we are able to quantify the degree-wise representation energy, and show that in some cases, substantial amounts of information processing capacity may reside in low-energy modes that are vulnerable to experimental noise. These results suggest that useful reservoir computation depends not only on dimensionality expansion, but also on the geometric organization of task-relevant information, with direct implications on physical reservoir computers.

</details>

### [7] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided NSGA-II Enhanced with Gray Relational Coefficient for Multi-Objective Optimization: Application to NASDAQ Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16194)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Qinxu Ding,Ding Ding,Siying Zhu,Jing Ren,Yue Wang,Chong Hui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合强化学习智能体与灰色关联度的改进NSGA-II算法（RL-NSGA-II-GRC），通过自适应参数控制和GRC锦标赛选择算子，在基准测试和纳斯达克投资组合优化中实现了约5%的收敛性提升，生成了平滑且密集的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标优化方法在处理风险与收益的权衡时，收敛性和Pareto前沿的多样性不足，缺乏自适应参数调节机制。

Method: 将RL智能体集成到NSGA-II框架中，通过超体积、可行性和多样性指标在线自适应调整进化参数；同时引入灰色关联度（GRC）增强二元锦标赛选择算子，综合考虑支配等级、拥挤距离和与理想点的接近度来排序父代个体。

Result: 在Kursawe和CONSTR基准上，相比NSGA-II收敛性分别提升约5.8%和4.4%；在纳斯达克投资组合应用中生成了平滑密集的有效前沿，最大夏普比组合年化夏普比为1.92。

Conclusion: RL-NSGA-II-GRC方法在收敛性和前沿分布质量上优于传统NSGA-II，能够为不同风险偏好的投资者提供可操作的投资组合决策支持。

Abstract: In modern financial markets, decision-makers increasingly rely on quantitative methods to navigate complex trade-offs among multiple, often conflicting objectives. This paper addresses constrained multi-objective optimization (MOO) with an application to portfolio optimization for minimizing risk and maximizing return. To address existing gaps, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-guided non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II) enhanced with gray relational coefficients (GRC), termed RL-NSGA-II-GRC, which combines an RL agent controller and GRC-based selection to improve convergence and diversity of Pareto fronts. The agent adapts evolutionary parameters online using metrics of hypervolume, feasibility, and diversity, while the GRC tournament operator ranks parents via a unified score considering dominance rank, crowding distance, and proximity to ideal reference. We evaluate the framework on the Kursawe and CONSTR benchmarks and a NASDAQ portfolio application. On the benchmarks, RL-NSGA-II-GRC achieves convergence improvements of about 5.8% and 4.4% over NSGA-II, while preserving well-distributed non-dominated solutions. In the portfolio application, it produces a smooth, densely populated efficient frontier supporting identification of the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio (annualized Sharpe =1.92) and utility-optimal portfolios for different risk-aversion levels. The main contributions are three-fold: 1) we propose an RL-NSGA-II-GRC method integrating an RL agent into the evolutionary framework to adaptively control parameters via generational feedback; 2) we design a GRC-enhanced binary tournament operator providing a comprehensive indicator to guide the search toward the Pareto front; 3) we demonstrate, on benchmark MOO and a NASDAQ case study, that the method delivers improved convergence and well-populated frontiers supporting actionable insights.

</details>


### [8] [DocOCR-Eval: A Correction-Based Framework for OCR Tool Selection Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16203)
*Zihan Xu,Puzhen Wu,Lawrence Chun Man Lau,Wei Liu,Sirui Li,Yifan Peng,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种OCR引擎和多模态大语言模型在扫描文档解析任务中的文本识别性能，并提出了一个无标注的自动评估框架DocOCR-Eval，通过三阶段校正与排序策略实现对OCR工具的有效选择，实验表明该方法在标签稀缺场景下具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR引擎和MLLMs种类繁多，但在标签稀缺的实际场景下，如何为特定文档集合选择最合适的解析方案仍是一大挑战。

Method: 提出了DocOCR-Eval无标注评估框架，采用三阶段校正与排序策略，通过聚合多个MLLM的输出逐步提升与基于标注排序的一致性，从而无需真实标签即可近似评估OCR工具性能。

Result: 聚合多个MLLM可以逐步改善与标注基准排序的对齐；在标签有限的真实场景下，能够实现可靠的OCR工具选择。

Conclusion: DocOCR-Eval框架无需人工标注即可有效评估和选择OCR工具，为不同文档集合的实际部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Document parsing is a foundational step for document understanding tasks such as visual question answering and key information extraction, as it transforms unstructured scanned images into structured representations by extracting textual, visual, and layout information. While numerous Optical Character Recognition (OCR) engines and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been developed for this purpose, selecting an appropriate document parsing solution for a given document collection remains challenging, particularly in label-scarce settings. In this work, we conduct a systematic evaluation of text recognition performance across a diverse set of OCR engines and state-of-the-art MLLMs on multiple scanned document benchmarks spanning different domains and languages. Motivated by the limited contextual reasoning capabilities of many OCR engines and the high cost of manual annotations, we propose DocOCR-Eval, an annotation-free evaluation framework for automatic OCR assessment and selection. DocOCR-Eval employs a three-staged correction and ranking strategy to approximate annotation-based tool ordering without ground-truth labels. We show that aggregating across multiple MLLMs progressively improves alignment with annotation-based rankings. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that reliable OCR tool selection can be achieved in realistic, label-limited settings, providing practical guidance for deploying document parsing systems across diverse real-world document collections.

</details>


### [9] [Fully-sensorized smart-eyewear platform for on-device Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16222)
*Andrea Giudici,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Mario Caliò,Aurelio Teliti,Giacomo Gervasoni,Diana Trojaniello,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARGO智能眼镜平台，基于STM32N6微控制器及其集成NPU实现本地化机器学习，通过软硬件与AI协同设计部署优化后的YOLOv11模型用于实时城市障碍物识别，在2.483 MB内存占用下达到24的mAP50-95，以200 mAh电池实现约113分钟续航，验证了高性能、隐私保护且社会可接受的可穿戴辅助设备的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜方案多依赖云计算，存在延迟高、隐私风险大等问题；同时需要兼顾佩戴舒适性、计算性能与能效，三者难以平衡。因此亟需一种端侧智能穿戴平台，在本地完成高性能推理，同时满足低功耗和小型化要求。

Method: 采用STM32N6微控制器及其集成NPU作为核心计算单元；设计Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA)架构改进以实现与NPU的高效适配；部署经过优化的YOLOv11模型；融合RGB摄像头、ToF传感器、麦克风及环境传感器的多模态传感套件；在WOTR数据集上训练模型；进行硬件、固件与AI的协同设计。

Result: 模型内存占用仅2.483 MB，mAP50-95达24；平台以200 mAh电池实现约113分钟连续续航；实现10 FPS的实时推理帧率。

Conclusion: ARGO平台证明了在严格资源受限的穿戴式设备上实现高性能、隐私保护型边缘AI的可行性，同时说明了竞争性边缘AI解决方案日益需要紧密耦合、多学科协同的设计方法。

Abstract: This paper presents ARGO, a smart eyewear platform designed to bridge ergonomic comfort, high computational throughput, and energy efficiency. Unlike cloud-dependent solutions, ARGO leverages the STM32N6 microcontroller and its integrated Neural Processing Unit (NPU) to enable on-device machine learning, minimizing latency and preserving user privacy through local data processing. The primary contribution lies in the holistic co-design of hardware, firmware, and artificial intelligence, centered on the deployment of an optimized YOLOv11 model for real-time urban obstacle recognition. To ensure compatibility with the target NPU, we introduce Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA), an architectural refinement that enables efficient accelerator execution while preserving the original computational logic. The model is trained on the Walking On The Road (WOTR) dataset, and the final deployed configuration achieves an mAP50-95 of 24 under strict memory constraints, with a memory footprint of only 2.483 MB. The platform integrates a multimodal sensor suite, RGB cameras, Time-of-Flight sensors, microphones, and ambient sensors, and delivers 10 FPS at a continuous autonomy of ~113 minutes on a 200 mAh battery. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a high-performance, privacy-preserving, and socially acceptable assistive device, and highlight how competitive edge AI solutions increasingly demand tightly integrated, multidisciplinary co-design approaches.

</details>


### [10] [LLM Unlearning for Cyber Defense: A Survey on Methods, Challenges, and Emerging Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16227)
*Ruppikha Sree Shankar,Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Troy Paulus Asia,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLM）遗忘（unlearning）领域的研究，聚焦于基于梯度的遗忘方法，探讨这些方法究竟是真正移除知识，还是仅阻止模型在常规提示下表达知识，并分析了该领域面临的安全、鲁棒性和可验证遗忘等核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在安全关键系统中广泛应用，但其无法遗忘的特性导致隐私泄露、版权侵权、有害知识保留和监管不合规等严重安全风险。由于重训练百亿参数模型计算上不可行，且知识以分布式、纠缠方式编码在参数中而非局部化，LLM遗忘成为主要的网络安全防御手段，但当前方法是否能真正移除知识仍是未解决的问题。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，以安全、鲁棒性和可验证遗忘为视角，重点考察基于梯度的遗忘方法（gradient-based methods），分析其因与现有训练流程兼容且可扩展至百亿参数模型而主导该领域的原因及局限性。

Result: 论文指出LLM遗忘领域存在核心未解问题：当前方法是否真正移除知识，还是仅抑制模型在常规提示下的知识表达。基于梯度的方法虽占据主导地位，但其有效性、可验证性和安全性仍有待深入检验。

Conclusion: LLM遗忘是应对大模型安全风险的关键防御手段，但现有方法（尤其是基于梯度的方法）在知识移除的真实性、可验证性和鲁棒性方面尚不成熟，需要进一步研究以确保可靠的遗忘机制。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in security-critical systems across healthcare, finance, education, and decision support, yet their inability to forget creates serious cybersecurity, privacy, and safety risks. Sensitive personal information, copyrighted material, hazardous domain knowledge, and memorized training data remain encoded across billions of parameters long after deployment, leaving models vulnerable to extraction, jailbreak attacks, membership inference, and regulatory non-compliance. Real-world incidents, from chatbots regenerating private information to fabricated legal citations producing direct legal and financial cost, place the problem at the center of the emerging-threats landscape rather than the realm of speculation. Because retraining billion-parameter models on revised corpora is computationally infeasible, and because knowledge within an LLM is distributed and entangled across parameters rather than localized to identifiable units, LLM unlearning has emerged as the principal cyber defense response, aiming to remove or suppress targeted knowledge from a trained model without retraining and without eroding what the model should still know. A central question, however, remains unresolved. Do current methods genuinely remove knowledge, or do they only stop the model from expressing it under ordinary prompting conditions? This survey examines LLM unlearning through the lens of security, robustness, and verifiable forgetting, with primary focus on gradient-based methods, which have come to dominate the field due to their compatibility with existing training pipelines and their scalability to billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [11] [Operator-Aware Mixed-Precision Tolerance Calibration for Tensor Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16228)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对GPU云上26个条目、2种数据类型的8,076次运行结果进行逐元素误差分布挖掘，提出了一种基于内核自身正确实现来校准绝对容差的方法。相比人工设定的容差，该方法能大幅收紧阈值（最大收紧达2,184倍），并将bug检测召回率从73.2%提升至82.4%（绝对提升9.3个百分点），同时仅引入1.1个百分点的假阳性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前张量核正确性测试中使用的固定形状、全接近式检查依赖于人工选择的绝对和相对容差，这些阈值在测试集中被复制粘贴且很少被重新审视，缺乏基于实际数据分布的客观依据。

Method: 从累积的云GPU运行中收集gpuemu语料库中26个条目、2种数据类型的8,076行结果，挖掘每个测试用例的元素级误差分布，并依据内核在正确实现下观察到的误差来校准绝对容差。

Result: 校准后的容差比手工设定的更严格，最大收紧幅度为attention_triton fp16的2,184倍。在7个LLM风格的有bug变体上，bug检测召回率从73.2%（1,805/2,467）提升至82.4%（2,034/2,467），绝对增益9.3个百分点（新增229个检测）。正确控制案例中假阳性从0增至20/1,882（+1.1个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基于内核正确实现观察到的误差分布来校准容差，可以显著提升bug检测能力，同时仅带来可接受的假阳性增加，该方法比人工设定阈值更合理有效。

Abstract: Most tensor-kernel correctness tests go through a fixed-shape all close-style check with hand-picked absolute and relative tolerances. The thresholds are copied across the corpus and rarely revisited. We mine the element-wise error distribution of every test case from accumulated cloud GPU runs across the 26-entry gpuemu corpus and 2 dtypes (8,076 result rows). We then ask one empirical question: what absolute tolerance would the kernel itself, observed under its correct implementation, justify?
  The answer is much tighter than the current hand-picked atol. The largest tightening is attention_triton fp16 at $2{,}184\times$. Restricted to the seven LLM-style buggy variants for which the corpus ships a paired correct counterpart, calibrated per-(op, dtype) tolerances raise bug-detection recall from 73.2% (1,805 of 2,467) to 82.4% (2,034 of 2,467), an absolute gain of 9.3 percentage points (+229 new detections). The control false-positive count rises from 0 to 20 out of 1,882 correct-control cases (+1.1 percentage points).

</details>


### [12] [RouteCost: A Production-Inspired Multi-Stage Framework for Pre-Order Shipping Cost Estimation in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16230)
*Xianling Zeng,Zihan Yu,Sichen Zhao,Yalun Qi,Zhiming Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RouteCost框架，将运输成本估算分解为需求预测、基准定价、残差修正和包裹合并推断四个阶段，在超过25万订单数据上提升了预测质量和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商中准确的运输成本估算对价格展示、利润规划和转化率至关重要，而静态查找方法和单一回归模型无法捕捉距离、需求组合、计费重量、体积定价、附加费触发和包裹合并等复杂因素引起的成本变化。

Method: 提出RouteCost多阶段框架：(1)时间感知的需求预测；(2)基于费率卡的基准定价；(3)第二阶段残差修正；(4)基于代理变量的包裹合并推断。最后通过路线加权期望聚合得到产品级运输成本预测。

Result: 在超过250,000个订单、260种产品和18个月的订单历史数据上，该框架提升了预测质量和整体校准性，同时保持了路线级别的可解释性。

Conclusion: RouteCost通过分解式多阶段建模有效解决了运输成本估算中的复杂变异性问题，兼顾了预测准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate pre-order shipping cost estimation is important in e-commerce because it affects price presentation, margin planning, and conversion. In practice, shipping cost is shaped not only by distance but also by destination demand mix, billable weight, dimensional pricing, surcharge triggers, and latent operational effects such as shipment consolidation. Static lookup methods therefore miss important sources of variation, while monolithic regressors may exploit strong but non-causal correlations. We propose RouteCost, a production-inspired multi-stage framework that decomposes the problem into time-aware demand forecasting, fee-card-informed baseline pricing, Stage 2 residual correction, and proxy-based box-consolidation inference. Route-level cost estimates are aggregated through a route-weighted expectation formulation to produce product-level shipping cost predictions. Across over 250,000 orders, 260 products, and 18 months of order history, the framework improves predictive quality and aggregate calibration while preserving route-level interpretability.

</details>


### [13] [Orthogonal Gradient Constraints Shape Noisy-Label Memorization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16231)
*Richard Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为OrthoGrad的几何干预方法，即在优化器更新时移除每个权重梯度中平行于当前权重向量的分量。实验表明，在小数据量的MNIST上，该方法能提升CNN测试精度并减少对损坏标签的拟合；但在大数据量下效果减弱。在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18上，OrthoGrad能改变记忆轨迹，但无法完全阻止对噪声标签的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络能够拟合被损坏的训练标签，这使噪声标签学习成为研究记忆驱动过拟合的有用场景。大多数正则化方法修改目标函数、架构或数据分布；本文转而研究优化器更新本身的几何干预，即OrthoGrad方法。

Method: OrthoGrad方法在优化器更新时，移除每个权重梯度中平行于当前权重向量的分量，即对梯度进行正交投影，使其仅保留与权重向量垂直的成分。

Result: 在MNIST小数据量场景下，OrthoGrad对CNN的测试精度提升最明显，同时减少了损坏标签的拟合。机制诊断表明，当原始梯度包含较大的径向分量时该投影效果最强；在大数据量场景下，梯度已经与权重近似正交，故效果减弱。CIFAR-10 ResNet-18实验显示该方法能改变记忆轨迹，但无法阻止最终的噪声标签记忆。

Conclusion: 正交更新约束可作为研究学习动力学的有用诊断工具，但OrthoGrad的效果具有场景依赖性，并非通用的正则化方法。

Abstract: Modern neural networks can fit corrupted training labels, making noisy-label learning a useful setting for studying memorization-driven overfitting. Most regularization methods modify the objective, architecture, or data distribution; here we instead study a geometric intervention on the optimizer update itself. We evaluate OrthoGrad, which removes the component of each weight gradient parallel to the current weight vector, in noisy-label image classification. On MNIST with small-data regimes, OrthoGrad improves test accuracy most clearly for CNNs while reducing corrupted-label fitting. Mechanism diagnostics based on weight norms and gradient-weight cosine similarity suggest that the projection has the strongest effect when the raw gradient contains a nontrivial radial component, and becomes weaker in larger-data regimes where gradients are already nearly orthogonal to weights. Additional CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 experiments show that the method can alter memorization trajectories but does not prevent eventual noisy-label memorization. These results support orthogonal update constraints as a useful diagnostic for studying learning dynamics, while showing that OrthoGrad is regime-dependent rather than universally regularizing.

</details>


### [14] [From Weights to Words: Expressing and Editing Preference Model Inferences in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16232)
*Zachary Wojtowicz,Ayush Nayak,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"weights to words"方法，能从高维选择数据中自动发现以自然语言描述的偏好维度，同时解决偏好学习中的欠确定性和不透明性问题。实验表明，该方法能提升预测准确性，且用户更偏好其推断结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从高维选择数据中推断人类偏好存在两个根本挑战：一是选择项在多个维度上同时存在差异，导致难以确定哪些因素真正驱动了决策（欠确定性）；二是统计学习算法的不透明性，使得人类无法检查、质疑或纠正模型错误。

Method: 提出"weights to words"方法，自动从选择问题数据中发现一组领域相关的偏好维度，每个维度用自然语言描述并与模型表示空间中的向量配对。这些维度可用于将归因集中到少数有意义的因素上，并以自然语言外化模型推断，支持用户实时检查和编辑。

Result: 在四个领域（道德困境、电影、葡萄酒、自由形式LLM响应）定性展示了方法的多样性。两个预注册人类实验（道德困境N=450，电影选择N=449）表明：(1)将偏好模型向学到的基正则化可提高预测准确性；(2)融入参与者的结构化编辑进一步提升准确性。在头对头比较中，参与者更偏好该方法推断的偏好轮廓并认可其预测更准确。

Conclusion: "weights to words"方法有效解决了偏好学习中的欠确定性和不透明性问题，既能提高预测准确率，又能让用户理解和编辑模型推断，在人机协作的偏好学习中具有重要价值。

Abstract: The growing use of statistical learning algorithms to infer human preferences from high-dimensional choice data runs up against a fundamental challenge: choice alternatives typically differ in many ways simultaneously, so it is generally unclear which factors actually drove an observed decision and should be credited as preferences. Compounding this problem, the opacity of these methods leaves human operators unable to inspect, contest, or correct models when they err. We introduce \emph{weights to words}, a method that takes a dataset of choice problems as input and automatically discovers a collection of domain-relevant preference dimensions, each described in natural language and paired with a vector in the model's representational space. These dimensions address both under-determination and opacity: they can be applied to concentrate attribution on a small set of meaningful factors, and they can externalize the model's inferences in natural language so that users can inspect and edit them in real time. We first qualitatively illustrate the method's versatility on four diverse domains: moral dilemmas, movies, wines, and free-form LLM responses. We then report two pre-registered human-subjects experiments, on moral dilemmas ($N=450$) and movie selection ($N=449$), that demonstrate its benefits for learning preference models: (1) regularizing a preference model toward the learned basis increases prediction accuracy on held-out choices, and (2) incorporating participants' structured edits further improves accuracy. In head-to-head comparisons, participants prefer the method's inferred preference profiles and endorse its predictions as more accurate.

</details>


### [15] [Token-Level Cross-Modal Transformer with Contrastive Multi-Task Learning for Breast Cancer Subtype Classification and Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16233)
*Suxing Liu Byungwon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态融合方法，通过细粒度token级交互、结构化token交换和联合优化目标，解决癌症亚型分类和生存预测中现有方法的三项局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在整合异构基因组和临床数据用于癌症亚型分类和生存预测时存在三个关键问题：将每种模态视为整体特征向量（缺乏细粒度token级交互）、跨模态融合仅通过线性加权或后期平均（缺乏结构化token交换）、以及生存预测和分类目标独立优化（缺少联合正则化信号）。

Method: 提出一种新的多模态融合框架，实现：(1) 细粒度token级跨模态交互；(2) 结构化token交换机制替代线性加权或后期平均；(3) 联合优化分类和生存预测目标，共享正则化信号。

Result: 该方法在癌症亚型分类和生存预测任务上优于现有基线方法，验证了token级交互、结构化交换和联合优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解决现有方法的三个关键局限性，该多模态融合框架为精准肿瘤学中的癌症亚型分类和生存预测提供了一种更有效的联合建模方案。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous genomic and clinical modalities for joint cancer subtype classification and survival prediction remains a key challenge in precision oncology. Existing approaches suffer from three limitations: (1) they treat each modality as a monolithic feature vector, precluding fine-grained token-level interactions across modalities; (2) cross-modal fusion is typically performed through linear weighting or late averaging rather than structured token exchange; and (3) survival and classification objectives are optimized independently, missing a joint regularization signal.

</details>


### [16] [HantaWatch: Federated Learning for Hantavirus Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16234)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HantaWatch是一个联邦学习框架，支持实验室和监测站点在不共享原始数据的情况下协同训练基于序列的汉坦病毒基因组监测模型，集成了k-mer特征提取、源感知联邦客户端构建、自适应DU-FedProx优化和预测分流等功能，在二分类和多分类任务中展现了良好的性能，可作为去中心化监测的决策支持层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉坦病毒基因组监测面临序列数据分布不均、非独立同分布的源异质性以及专家审核能力有限等挑战，现有方法难以在保护数据隐私的前提下实现有效的协同监测。

Method: 提出HantaWatch框架，采用联邦学习架构，包含k-mer特征提取、源感知联邦客户端构建、自适应DU-FedProx优化算法、监测特定模型选择以及预测分流机制；将模型输出转换为风险评分、置信度估计、不确定性标记和排序后的专家审核优先级。

Result: 在二分类和多分类任务上，HantaWatch支持高风险筛查、暴发关联预测、进化枝分类和临床综合征分类，在预测性能、假阴性风险和更新稳定性之间取得了平衡。

Conclusion: HantaWatch为去中心化汉坦病毒监测提供了一种实用的联邦决策支持层，能够在不替代实验室或公共卫生解释的前提下辅助专家优先级排序。

Abstract: Hantavirus genomic surveillance is limited by the distribution of sequence data, non-IID source heterogeneity, and constrained expert-review capacity. We propose HantaWatch, a federated learning framework that enables laboratories and surveillance sites to collaboratively train sequence-based models without sharing raw data. HantaWatch integrates k-mer feature extraction, source-aware federated client construction, adaptive DU-FedProx optimization, surveillance-specific model selection, and prediction-only triage. Experiments on binary and multi-class tasks show that HantaWatch supports high-risk screening, outbreak-associated prediction, clade classification, and clinical-syndrome categorization while balancing predictive performance, false-negative risk, and update stability. The framework converts model output into risk scores, confidence estimates, uncertainty flags, and ranked expert-review priorities. HantaWatch therefore provides a practical federated decision-support layer for decentralized Hantavirus surveillance, supporting expert prioritization without replacing laboratory or public-health interpretation.

</details>


### [17] [Discovery by Dreaming: Cross-Domain Recombination in Artificial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16256)
*Oliver Zahn,James Evans,David Eagleman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过神经网络微调（DREAMS）和符号引擎（SAPIENCE）两个系统证明，跨领域重组（类比梦境）而非领域内复述是产生创造性发现的关键，记忆巩固的真正价值在于发现而非记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统记忆巩固理论中"防止遗忘"的核心假设，提出记忆巩固的量化价值在于跨经验的重组性回放（recombinatory replay），并试图跨架构验证这一原则的通用性。

Method: 在两个架构无关的系统上实现重组性回放机制：① LoRA微调流水线（DREAMS，神经网络臂）② 符号引擎回放结构化知识对象（SAPIENCE，符号臂）。同时设置领域内复述作为对照，并对比上下文提示（in-context）效果以排除prompt伪影。

Result: 符号臂发现新颖跨领域连接达85.7%（+21 pp）；神经臂整体提升+5.64 pp，在明确需要跨领域迁移的子任务（如GSM8K未见数学推理）上提升达+14.5 pp。而将相同材料作为上下文提示给671B参数模型反而逆转增益，证明该效应是权重本身的属性。

Conclusion: 记忆巩固的核心功能不是防止遗忘，而是跨领域重组产生发现；该原则具有底物通用性（substrate-general），可追踪真实科学发现；提出了可证伪的海马体记录预测以区分重组与复述。

Abstract: Dreams splice together people, places, and times that never met. Neuroscience suggests this recombination is not noise, but a function driving insight and creative discovery. This reframes memory consolidation: rather than merely defending against forgetting, its measurable value lies in recombining knowledge across experiences that have not yet co-occurred. We test this directly by isolating the recombinatory-replay mechanism and implementing it in two architecturally unrelated systems: a LoRA fine-tuning pipeline (DREAMS) and a symbolic engine replaying structured knowledge objects (SAPIENCE). Both systems converge on the same finding: cross-domain consolidation creates value, while within-domain rehearsal does not. The symbolic arm surfaces novel cross-domain connections at 85.7%, a +21 percentage point (pp) gain over baseline. The neural arm improves overall by +5.64 pp, but on subtasks explicitly requiring cross-domain transfer (like unseen math reasoning on GSM8K), gains reach +14.5 pp. This effect is a genuine property of the weights--not a prompt artifact--as prepending the same material in-context to a 671B-parameter model actually reverses the gain. We validate this prediction against documented discoveries across 50,000 real papers and state a falsifiable hippocampal-recording prediction to distinguish recombination from rehearsal. Ultimately, this principle is substrate-general, tracking real discovery at scale. Reading the literature teaches a model to recall what it has seen, but producing discovery requires a separate offline phase that recombines knowledge across domains--the computational analog of dreaming. Consolidation is not for remembering, but for discovering.

</details>

*Narayan Schuetz,Yuze Bai,Lianggang Pan,Edgar Eggert,Favour Nerrise,Juan Delgado-SanMartin,Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Mohammad Asadi,Anders Johnson,Paul Schmiedmayer,Dennis Wang,Allan Lawrie,Daniel Seung Kim,Xin Liu,Akshay Paruchuri,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley,Kelly W. Zhang*
